        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "Go and start working on those Low Voltage components!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "Dear God...",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "This... is a Bucket.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "The final task in the Metallurgy Age is creating a Bucket. Unlike the wooden bucket and other fluid containers you've been using until now, the Bucket can be used to carry Lava and move around source blocks of other fluids.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "Bucket Fusion Reactor",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "Bucket Components",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "With the Black Steel Anvil you'll now be able to work Black Steel Ingots into different items.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "You've also now opened up a massive new section in the Early Automation chapter related to high pressure Steam Power!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "From here, you can either dive straight into industrialization or finish up the last tier of anvils if you'd like. You can't avoid colored steel forever, though!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "Steam power is within your grasp",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "T5 Anvil: Black Steel",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "With the Steel Anvil, you'll be able to work Steel Ingots into different forms.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "Steel tools and equipment have a significant boost over their bronze and wrought iron counterparts, so they're worth making if your existing ones are about to break. You'll be needing to create a lot of alloys soon, so some new mining equipment would be a wise investment.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "tfc:field_guide tfc:mechanics/steel",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "A huge leap from wrought iron",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "T4 Anvil: Steel",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "With the Wrought Iron Anvil you'll be able to work Wrought Iron Ingots into different items such as Sheets, Tool heads, etc.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "Wrought Iron tools and armor isn't much of an improvement over Bronze, but it has far more durability.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "Creating the next anvil is going to be a significant step up in difficulty, so this is a good point to settle down and explore some of the other things this modpack has to offer.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "tfc:field_guide tfc:tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "Waterwheels, Windmills and more Greate",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "T3 Anvil: Wrought Iron",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "To work metal into more than just double ingots, you'll need to use an anvil of the same tier or better. Working on an anvil can be a little bit complicated, but there's an entry in the TFG Tips section of the field guide that goes into more detail.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "Bronze also marks the start of automation, where you'll begin to harness mechanical power!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "Basic mechanical power starts here",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {

          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "Obtain at least 14 Ingots of any Bronze type, then weld them together in the Copper Anvil.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "The Tier 2 Anvil can be made from Bismuth Bronze, Bronze or Black Bronze, but not a mixture of the three.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "I told you to get used to it",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "or(item(gtceu:bronze_double_ingot)item(gtceu:black_bronze_double_ingot)item(gtceu:bismuth_bronze_double_ingot))",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "{quests.tasktype.item.any} #forge:double_ingots/bronze",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "Welding Bronze",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "The Bloomery is a Multiblock from TerraFirmaCraft that allows you to create Wrought Iron. Melting iron in a forge will produce Cast Iron, which is brittle and nowhere near as strong or durable as Wrought Iron.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "The Bloomery is expensive, so it will be worth investing some time into setting up automatic plate production and ore processing.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "The Field Guide contains a detailed entry on how the Bloomery works and the correct ratios of iron and fuel that it needs.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "tfc:field_guide tfc:mechanics/bloomery",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "Required if you seek the Iron Age.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "The Blast Furnace is a multiblock structure that allows you to create Pig Iron. Placing a combination of Iron, Flux and fuel (see the adjacent quest) inside the Blast Furnace produces Pig Iron, which can be worked into Steel.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "In order for the Blast Furnace to reach its required high temperatures, it will need its own Bellows, used to force air through a Tuyere which you place inside the blast furnace.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "The Field Guide contains an entry on how the Blast Furnace works and how to build one.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "tfc:field_guide tfc:tfg_tips/blast_furnace_tips",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "Then I started Blastin'",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "ftbfiltersystem:item_tag(tfc:tuyeres)",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        }
      ],
      "aliases": []
    },
    {
      "id": "tfc:prospecting",
      "label": "Prospecting",
      "origin": "namespace_generated",

          "kind": "guide_page",

      "semantic_evidence": [
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/prospecting",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/prospecting.json",
          "text": "You remembered where you picked up those small metal nuggets, right? Finding additional ores may require extensive exploration and mining. You should become very familiar with ores and minerals. If you need a specific resource, you must find the rock type it spawns in either under your feet or across the world.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Prospecting",
          "item_ref_count": 12,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/prospecting",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/prospecting.json",
          "text": "When picking up small nuggets becomes unsatisfying, it is time to start prospecting to find ore veins: - Small nuggets, surface buds, and surface rocks occur when an ore vein is located below. If you find the center of a group of nuggets, it's likely that the vein is beneath you. - Exposed ore can occur in cliffs and water bodies, which may be seen from farther away.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Prospecting",
          "item_ref_count": 12,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/prospecting",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/prospecting.json",
          "text": "Prospector's Pick",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.title",
          "label": "Prospecting",
          "item_ref_count": 12,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/prospecting",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/prospecting.json",
          "text": "If you're looking for ore veins, and you can't find the vein by guessing, it's time to pull out the prospector's pick. If you are familiar with base prospector's picks in TFC you may be suprised to learn that they function very differently in TerraFirmaGreg",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Prospecting",
          "item_ref_count": 12,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/prospecting",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/prospecting.json",
          "text": "Right-clicking a prospector's pick in TFG will cast a search ray in your views direction. The rays width and depth is dependent on the tier of the pick noted in its tooltips. Depending on the tier of the prospector's pick can also give you a count of the ore quantities and even display an X-Ray indicator of the ore vein.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Prospecting",
          "item_ref_count": 12,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/prospecting",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/prospecting.json",
          "text": "The prospector's pick can display the following information on use: - In chat the pick can display the total quantity of the ore vein, the contents of the ore vein, and the quantity per ore-type of the ore vein. - colored steel prospector's picks can also display either a large X-Ray particle in the center of an ore vein, or paricle's per ore block",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Prospecting",
          "item_ref_count": 12,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/prospecting",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/prospecting.json",
          "text": "Prospector View",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.title",
          "label": "Prospecting",
          "item_ref_count": 12,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/prospecting",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/prospecting.json",
          "text": "Using a prospector's pick looks like this. The large \"!\" mark particle is from per-vein style x-ray. And the smaller ones are from per-block style x-ray.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "Prospecting",
          "item_ref_count": 12,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/prospecting",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/prospecting.json",
          "text": "To make a prospector's pick, you can knapp an unfired mold out of clay as shown above.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.6.text",
          "label": "Prospecting",
          "item_ref_count": 12,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/prospecting",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/prospecting.json",
          "text": "Once the mold has been knapped, it needs to be fired to create a propick head mold. To create the tool head, cast liquid metal into the mold.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.7.text",
          "label": "Prospecting",
          "item_ref_count": 12,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/prospecting",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/prospecting.json",
          "text": "A prospector's pick head can also be smithed out of an ingot of any tool metal on an Anvil. The prospector's pick is then created by crafting the tool head with a stick.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.8.text",
          "label": "Prospecting",
          "item_ref_count": 12,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/prospecting",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/prospecting.json",
          "text": "The majority of the stats and charecteristics of the prospector's picks can be adjustable in your server config file.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.9.text",
          "label": "Prospecting",
          "item_ref_count": 12,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/prospecting",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/prospecting.json",
          "text": "Digital Ore Prospector's",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.10.title",
          "label": "Prospecting",
          "item_ref_count": 12,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/prospecting",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/prospecting.json",
          "text": "Technology is now! And with it comes new ways to find your ore veins! Ore Prospector's are GregTech's solution to finding ore veins and fluid deposits in your world. The size and capabilities of the ore prospectors are determined by the voltage tier.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.10.text",
          "label": "Prospecting",
          "item_ref_count": 12,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/prospecting",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/prospecting.json",
          "text": "Ore Prospector",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.11.title",
          "label": "Prospecting",
          "item_ref_count": 12,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/prospecting",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/prospecting.json",
          "text": "An example of what you may see using the LuV ore prospector.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.11.text",
          "label": "Prospecting",
          "item_ref_count": 12,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 12
        },
        {

        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/buildinggadget",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/buildinggadget.json",
          "text": "Building Gadget",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Building Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/buildinggadget",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/buildinggadget.json",
          "text": "The building gadget makes building a large number of blocks all at once extremely easy! First, ensure the gadget has some energy. Second, Shift right-click a block in the world to specify the block to build with.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Building Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/buildinggadget",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/buildinggadget.json",
          "text": "You can choose your Building Mode by holding the settings key. Each mode is described in its appropriate entry in this book. The default mode is Build to Me. When looking at a block in the world, you'll see a ghostly image of the blocks that will be placed. If you're missing the required items or energy to place these blocks, they will have a red overlay. Right click to place the blocks as shown.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Building Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/buildinggadget",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/buildinggadget.json",
          "text": "Build Preview",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.title",
          "label": "Building Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/buildinggadget",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/buildinggadget.json",
          "text": "Seeing a preview of what will be built",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Building Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/buildinggadget",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/buildinggadget.json",
          "text": "When you right click, building will begin. Items will be taken out of your inventory to be placed into the world, and any blocks you don't have the necessary item for will be skipped. Don't forget you can undo if you make a mistake!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Building Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste.json",
          "text": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste.json",
          "text": "The copy paste gadget makes replicating structures far easier! To start, ensure you're in 'copy' mode in the settings menu. Right click one corner of a structure,and then shift-right click an opposing corner. If done correctly, a green outline will surround your structure.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste.json",
          "text": "Copying a structure",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste",

          "text": "Switch the gadget to 'paste' mode, and you'll see a ghostly preview of the structure you've copied. The block you right clicked during copying is the starting point of the structure. To place the structure in world, simply right click! All items necessary for building will be removed from your inventory, and any blocks you don't have items for will be skipped.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste.json",
          "text": "Paste Preview",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.title",
          "label": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste.json",
          "text": "Previewing a paste",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste.json",
          "text": "To see a list of the items you need, open the settings menu and choose the 'material list' option. This is a scrollable list showing what items you do or do not have, and can be sorted in multiple ways. Try it out!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",

          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste.json",
          "text": "Needed items",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",

          "text": "Its possible to make fine tuned adjustments to the copy area by opening the Adjust Placement window in the settings menu. From here, you can adjust the position of the start block and end block. Click the plus and minus buttons to see the green box change in real time. You can also swap to 'absolute' coordinates mode and enter in-game world coordinates instead.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.6.text",
          "label": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste.json",
          "text": "Adjusting Copy",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.7.title",
          "label": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste.json",
          "text": "Changing the area you are copying",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.7.text",
          "label": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste.json",
          "text": "Theres a similar option for paste mode. In this UI, you'll be adjusting the location of the starting block. Click the buttons and the render should update in-world in real time.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.8.text",
          "label": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",

          "text": "Adjusting Paste",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.9.title",
          "label": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste.json",
          "text": "Changing the location of the paste",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.9.text",
          "label": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste.json",
          "text": "Cut Paste Gadget",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Cut Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste.json",
          "text": "The cut paste gadget makes moving structures a breeze, and works just like the Copy Paste Gadget! To start, ensure you're in 'cut' mode in the settings menu. Right click one corner of a structure,and then shift-right click an opposing corner. If done correctly, a red outline will surround your structure.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Cut Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste.json",
          "text": "Cutting a structure",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Cut Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste.json",
          "text": "The structure will not be removed until you click the 'cut' button in the settings menu. Switch the gadget to 'paste' mode, and you'll see a ghostly preview of the structure you've copied. To place the structure in world, simply right click! No items are necessary for this, as its using the blocks you removed earlier.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Cut Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste.json",
          "text": "Paste Preview",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.title",
          "label": "Cut Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste.json",
          "text": "Previewing a paste",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Cut Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste.json",
          "text": "You can only have one structure stored on the gadget at a time. Placing it in the world will erased the stored information on the gadget. Be careful when pasting the structure, you only get one chance, exchange mode is recommended! If you attempt to cut a new area while data already exists, you'll be prompted to confirm. If you do this, the first set of data will be lost forever!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Cut Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste.json",
          "text": "All block-entity data will be moved along with the structure, including the contents of chests and block configurations. Much like the Copy Paste Gadget, you can modify the cut or paste positions with the appropriate settings menu option. See that chapter for details!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "Cut Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/destruction",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/destruction.json",
          "text": "Destruction Gadget",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Destruction Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 3
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/destruction",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/destruction.json",
          "text": "The destruction gadget makes clearing a large area very easy. Any blocks cleared will be voided!. First, open the settings menu and adjust the area. This area is with respect to the block (and block face) you are looking at. If you look at the top face of a block, 'up' will be relative to the player's screen.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Destruction Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 3
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/destruction",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/destruction.json",
          "text": "Destruction Gadget",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.title",
          "label": "Destruction Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 3
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/destruction",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/destruction.json",
          "text": "Destroying an area, click the arrow to see diffent pictures!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Destruction Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 3
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/destruction",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/destruction.json",
          "text": "Once you're satisfied with the settings, right click on a block to void all the highlighted blocks. They will be removed from the world. You may undo this action if you make a mistake. It will not remove items from your inventory to undo, as the gadget is just returning the voided blocks to the world.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Destruction Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 3
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/exchanginggadget",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/exchanginggadget.json",
          "text": "Exchanging Gadget",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Exchanging Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/exchanginggadget",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/exchanginggadget.json",
          "text": "The exchanging gadget can swap one set of blocks for another! It cannot, however, replace air blocks. Use a Building Gadget for that! First, ensure the gadget has some energy. Second, Shift right-click a block in the world to specify the block to exchange to.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Exchanging Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/exchanginggadget",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/exchanginggadget.json",
          "text": "You can choose your Exchanging Mode by holding the settings key. Each mode is described in its appropriate entry in this book. The default mode is Surface. When looking at a block in the world, you'll see a ghostly image of the blocks that will be placed over top of existing blocks. If you're missing the required items or energy to place these blocks, they will have a red overlay. Right click to exchange the blocks as shown.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Exchanging Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/exchanginggadget",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/exchanginggadget.json",
          "text": "Exchange Preview",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.title",
          "label": "Exchanging Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/exchanginggadget",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/exchanginggadget.json",
          "text": "Seeing a preview of what will be exchanged",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Exchanging Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/exchanginggadget",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/exchanginggadget.json",
          "text": "When you right click, exchanging will begin. Items will be taken out of your inventory to be placed into the world, and any blocks you don't have the necessary item for will be skipped. Items for the removed blocks will be returned to you. Note: You can enchant this tool with silk touch. Without it grass will return dirt, etc.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Exchanging Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/exchanginggadget",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/exchanginggadget.json",
          "text": "You can undo with the exchanging gadget, but you must have the necessary items. For example, if you exchanged grass into stone bricks, you must have grass in your inventory to undo this exchange. Don't forget to apply silk touch if desired!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Exchanging Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/mech_anchor",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/mech_anchor.json",
          "text": "Anchor allows you to 'lock in' the block you're looking at, so you can freely move or look around. There is a hotkey for this, or you can access it from the settings menu. You can right click at any time to build the structure when it is anchored. Note: Changing range may not take effect until you remove and replace the anchor.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Anchor",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 2
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/mech_anchor",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/mech_anchor.json",
          "text": "Anchor your render!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Anchor",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 2
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/mech_bindinv",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/mech_bindinv.json",
          "text": "Bind Inventory",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Bind Inventory",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 4
        }
      ],
      "aliases": []
    },
    {
      "id": "tfc:rowboats",
      "label": "Rowboats",
      "origin": "namespace_generated",
      "confidence": 0.7,
      "support": 20,
      "evidence": [
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/rowboat",
