          "label": "Flora",
          "item_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 18
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora.json",
          "text": "Tall Water Plant",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.14.name",
          "label": "Flora",
          "item_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 18
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora.json",
          "text": "Tall water plants can grow with just the bottom block in water. Water Taro and Cattail can be broken with a Knife for Roots.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.14.text",
          "label": "Flora",
          "item_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 18
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora.json",
          "text": "Floating plants sit on top of the water. Boats will break them on contact.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.15.text",
          "label": "Flora",
          "item_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 18
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora.json",
          "text": "Kelp are twisting vines that grow underwater.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.16.text",
          "label": "Flora",
          "item_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 18
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora.json",
          "text": "Tree Kelp",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.17.name",
          "label": "Flora",
          "item_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 18
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora.json",
          "text": "Tree kelp grow into intricate trees underwater. The flowers can be harvested with a Knife.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.17.text",
          "label": "Flora",
          "item_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 18
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops.json",
          "text": "Soul soil may be tilled to form Soul Farmland. Crops work a little differently when you are Beneath. There are five nutrients: Death, Destruction, Decay, Sorrow, and Flame. Nutrients are viewed by holding a hoe, but will not appear unless they have a value greater than zero.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 7
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops.json",
          "text": "Crops have a preferred nutrient: Instead of consuming it, this is the nutrient they will add to over time. The crop will happily consume the other four nutrients in order to grow. Nether crops cannot die, but still need block light to grow.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 7
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops.json",
          "text": "The fertilizers are as follows: Sulfur: 20% decay, 10% flame Gunpowder: 60% Destruction, 10% Flame, 40% Death Ghast Tear: 30% Sorrow Blaze Powder: 20% Flame",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 7
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops.json",
          "text": "The fertilizers are also available in obtainable 'pure' forms from chest loot. Crafting these together yields Agonizing Fertilizer, which has them all in a neat package.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 7
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops.json",
          "text": "Crimson Roots (Decay) and Warped Roots (Destruction) are growable as crops. These are most useful as means of improving the nutrient quality of your soul farmland. Nether Wart (Death), found in fortresses, can also be grown as a crop.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 7
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops.json",
          "text": "The Gleamflower (Flame) can be grown as a crop, and also found in the world as a plant. It is useful as an infinite light source.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 7
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops.json",
          "text": "Ghost Peppers (Sorrow) are an edible vegetable that can be grown in the Beneath!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.6.text",
          "label": "Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 7
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/curious_blocks",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/curious_blocks.json",
          "text": "Curious Blocks",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Curious Blocks",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/curious_blocks",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/curious_blocks.json",
          "text": "Soul Clay",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.name",
          "label": "Curious Blocks",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/curious_blocks",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/curious_blocks.json",
          "text": "Soul Clay may be discovered in soul sand valleys. It is a source of regular clay.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Curious Blocks",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/curious_blocks",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/curious_blocks.json",
          "text": "Sulfur can be broken with a shovel. Be careful, though: iron-containing items (including steel) explode in contact with sulfur!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Curious Blocks",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/curious_blocks",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/curious_blocks.json",
          "text": "Cutting grass will yield you warped or crimson straw. This can be made into thatch, the normal way.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Curious Blocks",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/curious_blocks",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/curious_blocks.json",
          "text": "The blackstone aqueduct allows lava to be conducted at any distance.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Curious Blocks",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/curious_blocks",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/curious_blocks.json",
          "text": "Hellbricks are a building block that is also used for the Hellforge.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Curious Blocks",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/curious_blocks",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/curious_blocks.json",
          "text": "Blackstone bricks are made like regular bricks, and that includes needing to create the brick item.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "Curious Blocks",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/hellforge",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/hellforge.json",
          "text": "The Hellforge",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "The Hellforge",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/hellforge",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/hellforge.json",
          "text": "The Hellforge is a gratuitously large forge, with high capacity for fuel, products, and heating. It is constructed from Hellbricks, Anthracite, and Pyroxenite Aqueducts.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "The Hellforge",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/hellforge",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/hellforge.json",
          "text": "The Hellforge requires lava to flow in aqueducts in its four corners, as well as 21 hellbricks. The 3x3 center must be filled with Anthracite piles, which then may be lit to start the forge.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "The Hellforge",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/hellforge",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/hellforge.json",
          "text": "The item slots of the Hellforge may contain fuel, or things to be smelted; there is no distinction. Be careful what you put in! There are also slots for melting things, as with the charcoal forge. The lava meter on the screen indicates temperature (as well as the visual movement of its items in world).",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "The Hellforge",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/hellforge",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/hellforge.json",
          "text": "The Hellforge",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "The Hellforge",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/hellforge",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/hellforge.json",
          "text": "The Hellforge is a gratuitously large forge, with high capacity for fuel, products, and heating. It is constructed from Hellbricks, Cursecoal, and Blackstone Aqueducts.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "The Hellforge",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/hellforge",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/hellforge.json",
          "text": "The Hellforge requires lava to flow in aqueducts in its four corners, as well as 21 hellbricks. The 3x3 center must be filled with Cursecoal piles, which then may be lit to start the forge.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "The Hellforge",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/hellforge",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/hellforge.json",
          "text": "The item slots of the Hellforge may contain fuel, or things to be smelted; there is no distinction. Be careful what you put in! There are also slots for melting things, as with the charcoal forge. The lava meter on the screen indicates temperature (as well as the visual movement of its items in world).",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "The Hellforge",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals.json",
          "text": "Ores and Minerals",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Ores and Minerals",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals.json",
          "text": "The ores you can find Beneath are mostly similar to what you can find on the surface, but with a few changes. Consult the Ores in TFG category for exact details.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Ores and Minerals",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals.json",
          "text": "The most common rock type found is Migmatite. This deep metamorphic rock contains a wide variety of ores you'd normally find in other rock types on the surface.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Ores and Minerals",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals.json",
          "text": "In wetter areas, you may find Travertine. It is a sedimentary rock that can contain the same kind of ores as Migmatite.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Ores and Minerals",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals.json",
          "text": "Around the deepest level of the Beneath, the different rock types gradually all turn into Pyroxenite. This ultramafic igneous rock is extremely rich in ores, but this depth also comes with extreme heat, so be prepared!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Ores and Minerals",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals.json",
          "text": "Anthracite is a powerful fuel. It can be found in Migmatite in the upper half of the Beneath.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Ores and Minerals",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals.json",
          "text": "Ores and Minerals",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Ores and Minerals",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals.json",
          "text": "Beneath the overworld, you will still find ore veins to exploit. It is important to note that Netherrack does not collapse, like raw stone would. However, Blackstone, Nether Bricks, Cobblerack and miscellaneous other blocks do. Prospecting will still work Beneath the overworld.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Ores and Minerals",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals.json",
          "text": "Nether Gold",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.name",
          "label": "Ores and Minerals",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals.json",
          "text": "Nether Gold replaces netherrack, and is common. Beneath lava, there is more gold, and richer.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Ores and Minerals",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals.json",
          "text": "Cursecoal is a powerful fuel. It replaces netherrack above level 80.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Ores and Minerals",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals.json",
          "text": "Sylvite replaces basalt at any level.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Ores and Minerals",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals.json",
          "text": "Nether Quartz",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.name",
          "label": "Ores and Minerals",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/ores_and_minerals.json",
          "text": "Nether Quartz replaces netherrack at all levels, and is common.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Ores and Minerals",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 6
        }
      ],
      "aliases": []
    },

      "semantic_evidence": [
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/herbs_and_spices",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/herbs_and_spices.json",
          "text": "Herbs and Spices",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Herbs and Spices",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/herbs_and_spices",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/herbs_and_spices.json",
          "text": "In Firmalife, there are a number of small plants you can collect and grow on your own, which have cooking properties. They can all be found in the wild, and once harvested, may be cultivated in a Greenhouse, in which they consume 20 points of N, P, and K.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Herbs and Spices",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/herbs_and_spices",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/herbs_and_spices.json",
          "text": "Wild Basil grows between 12 and 25C, and 300 to 500mm of rainfall.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Herbs and Spices",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/herbs_and_spices",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/herbs_and_spices.json",
          "text": "Bay Laurel",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.name",
          "label": "Herbs and Spices",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/herbs_and_spices",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/herbs_and_spices.json",
          "text": "Wild Bay Laurel grows between 8 and 14C, and 25 to 225mm of rainfall.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Herbs and Spices",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/herbs_and_spices",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/herbs_and_spices.json",
          "text": "Wild Cardamom grows between 18 and 30C, and 350 to 500mm of rainfall.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Herbs and Spices",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/herbs_and_spices",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/herbs_and_spices.json",
          "text": "Wild Cilantro grows between 5 and 15C, and 0 to 150mm of rainfall.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Herbs and Spices",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/herbs_and_spices",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/herbs_and_spices.json",
          "text": "Wild Cumin grows between 6 and 20C, and 50 to 250mm of rainfall.",

          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/herbs_and_spices",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/herbs_and_spices.json",
          "text": "Wild Oregano grows between 5 and 13C, and 50 to 150mm of rainfall.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.6.text",
          "label": "Herbs and Spices",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/herbs_and_spices",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/herbs_and_spices.json",
          "text": "Wild Pimento grows between 15 and 30C, and 200 to 450mm of rainfall.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.7.text",
          "label": "Herbs and Spices",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/herbs_and_spices",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/herbs_and_spices.json",
          "text": "Wild Vanilla grows between 18 and 30C, and 200 to 500mm of rainfall.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.8.text",
          "label": "Herbs and Spices",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/herbs_and_spices",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/herbs_and_spices.json",
          "text": "Seed Balls can be used to plant different types of Grass. This can be useful after clearing your base for building. The recipe for the seed ball requires Compost and 4 Seeds.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.9.text",
          "label": "Herbs and Spices",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/herbs_and_spices",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/herbs_and_spices.json",
          "text": "A butterfly grass plant.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.10.text",
          "label": "Herbs and Spices",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/herbs_and_spices",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/herbs_and_spices.json",
          "text": "Butterfly grass will mature over time. When one reaches maturity, it has a chance to spread to surrounding blocks, or turn into something new. Butterfly grass blocks that have been spread by another grass block do not spread anymore. After Butterfly grass turns into another grass, you can pick them up with Shears.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.11.text",
          "label": "Herbs and Spices",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/herbs_and_spices",
          "source": "jar:Firmalife-1.20.1-2.1.27.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/herbs_and_spices.json",
          "text": "Herbs and Spices",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Herbs and Spices",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "recipe_ref_count": 2,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/herbs_and_spices",
          "source": "jar:Firmalife-1.20.1-2.1.27.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/herbs_and_spices.json",
          "text": "In Firmalife, there are a number of small plants you can collect and grow on your own, which have cooking properties. The easiest way to obtain these plants is with a Seed Ball. To use a Seed Ball, just to throw it, like a snowball. This will spawn Butterfly Grass in the area.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Herbs and Spices",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "recipe_ref_count": 2,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/herbs_and_spices",

          "text": "The recipe for the seed ball requires Compost and 4 Seeds.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",

          "recipe_ref_count": 2,
          "count": 6
        },

          "text": "A butterfly grass plant.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Herbs and Spices",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "recipe_ref_count": 2,
          "count": 6
        },
        {

          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/herbs_and_spices",
          "source": "jar:Firmalife-1.20.1-2.1.27.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/herbs_and_spices.json",
          "text": "Butterfly grass will mature over time. When one reaches maturity, it has a chance to spread to surrounding blocks, or turn into something new. Butterfly grass blocks that have been spread by another grass block do not spread anymore.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",

          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/herbs_and_spices",
          "source": "jar:Firmalife-1.20.1-2.1.27.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/herbs_and_spices.json",
          "text": "Basil is one of the plants that can be spawned by butterfly grass.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Herbs and Spices",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "recipe_ref_count": 2,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/herbs_and_spices",
          "source": "jar:Firmalife-1.20.1-2.1.27.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/herbs_and_spices.json",
          "text": "Basil leaves are used in pizza.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "Herbs and Spices",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "recipe_ref_count": 2,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel.json",
          "text": "Better Fuel",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Better Fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel.json",
          "text": "Cryo Freezer",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.title",
          "label": "Better Fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel.json",
          "text": "Fuel is hard to get; it requires venturing out to the ocean, looking for oil spouts. The Cryo Freezer introduces a new way to obtain a better fuel - Cryo Fuel. Cryo Fuel is much more efficient than refined fuel, allowing you to launch a rocket with just one bucket.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Better Fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel.json",
          "text": "It's created by collecting ice shards, which are found as an ore underground on cold planets. The ice shard then needs to be inserted into a Cryo Freezer, and it will convert to 25 mB of Cryo Fuel. Alternatively, you can convert ice, packed ice and blue ice to fuel. Once you've made at least two buckets (one for a launch and the other for a return trip), place the buckets in the rocket fuel slot.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Better Fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel.json",
          "text": "Cryo Fuel",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.title",
          "label": "Better Fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel.json",
          "text": "In addition to being an excellent fuel, cryo fuel has some other features. When placed, it will damage anything inside, similar to lava. It also freezes water, allowing for an infinite ice source.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Better Fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/detection",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/detection.json",
          "text": "Oxygen Sensor",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.title",
          "label": "Detection",
          "item_ref_count": 3,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 2
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/detection",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/detection.json",
          "text": "The oxygen sensor is a block that will emit a redstone signal when it detects oxygen. You can shift-right-click it to invert the signal. Despite its name, you can also change its detection type by right-clicking with a wrench. It has the option to detect oxygen, safe temperature, and normal gravity.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Detection",
          "item_ref_count": 3,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 2
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/energizer",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/energizer.json",
          "text": "The energizer is a machine capable of powering items and storing large amounts of energy. To charge an item, power the energizer and then right-click on the energizer with that item. It stores 2 million energy, making it capable of charging machines for a very long time. It also retains its energy when broken.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Energizer",
          "item_ref_count": 3,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 2
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/flags",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/flags.json",
          "text": "Flags are decorative blocks available in 16 colors. By default, when placed, they will display the face of the flag owner. They can be configured to display a custom image from a URL by right-clicking and entering the URL in the GUI. This will display the internet image onto the flag.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Flags",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 2
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/fluid_pipes",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/fluid_pipes.json",
          "text": "Fluid Pipes",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Fluid Pipes",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/fluid_pipes",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/fluid_pipes.json",
          "text": "Fluid Pipes",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.title",
          "label": "Fluid Pipes",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/fluid_pipes",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/fluid_pipes.json",
          "text": "Fluid pipes are similar to cables but with fluid transfer instead of energy transfer. To use them, craft some fluid pipes and a wrench. Then, right-click on one of the pipes with the wrench to cycle through the pipe's modes: 'none,' 'normal,' 'insert,' and 'extract.'",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Fluid Pipes",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/fluid_pipes",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/fluid_pipes.json",
          "text": "Pipes with 'insert' set will receive fluid from the network, while pipes with 'extract' set will pump fluid into the network.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Fluid Pipes",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/fluid_pipes",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/fluid_pipes.json",
          "text": "Like cables, fluid pipes also have a duct block for transfering fluids safely in and out of sealed structures.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Fluid Pipes",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/industrial_lamps",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/industrial_lamps.json",
          "text": "Industrial Lamps",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Industrial Lamps",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 2
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/industrial_lamps",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/industrial_lamps.json",
          "text": "Industrial Lamps",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.title",
          "label": "Industrial Lamps",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 2
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/industrial_lamps",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/industrial_lamps.json",
          "text": "Industrial lamps are available in all 16 colors, and in small and large sizes. By default, these will all emit normal white light; however, if you have Shimmer, each light will emit its respective color.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Industrial Lamps",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 2
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/netherite_space_suit",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/netherite_space_suit.json",
          "text": "Netherite Space Suit",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Netherite Space Suit",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 2,
          "count": 3
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/netherite_space_suit",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/netherite_space_suit.json",
          "text": "Netherite Space Suit",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.title",
          "label": "Netherite Space Suit",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 2,
          "count": 3
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/netherite_space_suit",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/netherite_space_suit.json",
          "text": "The netherite space suit is an upgraded version of the standard space suit, featuring permanent fire resistance, more protection and the ability to survive on Venus and Mercury. Before you travel to these planets, you must craft the netherite space suit armour or else you will burn to death.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Netherite Space Suit",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 2,
          "count": 3
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/sliding_doors",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/sliding_doors.json",
          "text": "Sliding Doors",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Sliding Doors",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/sliding_doors",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/sliding_doors.json",
          "text": "Sliding Doors",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.title",
          "label": "Sliding Doors",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/sliding_doors",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/sliding_doors.json",
          "text": "Sliding doors are large 3x3 doors. They seal oxygen when closed, making them great candidates for airlocks. They can be locked by shift-right-clicking them with a wrench. Locked doors can only be opened with redstone.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Sliding Doors",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/creating_plates",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/creating_plates.json",
          "text": "Creating Plates",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Creating Plates",
          "item_ref_count": 3,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 2
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/creating_plates",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/creating_plates.json",
          "text": "Once you've got energy, you'll want to craft a compressor. This machine can compress ingots and blocks into plates, which are needed to craft many things.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Creating Plates",
          "item_ref_count": 3,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 2
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/fuel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/fuel.json",
          "text": "Even after you place your rocket, you will not be able to fly just yet. To fly, you will need to fill the rocket with fuel.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/fuel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/fuel.json",
          "text": "Obtaining Fuel",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.title",
          "label": "Fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/fuel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/fuel.json",
          "text": "Oil is found in large amounts spouting over the ocean surface. Collect as much oil as you can, as oil is non-renewable, and you need a lot of it for each rocket launch.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/fuel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/fuel.json",
          "text": "Obtaining Fuel",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.title",
          "label": "Fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/fuel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/fuel.json",
          "text": "After collecting enough oil (at least six buckets for a launch and return), you will need to refine the oil into fuel. To do this, you will need to craft a fuel refinery. Power it, then place the oil in the left slot, and it will be refined into fuel.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/fuel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/fuel.json",
          "text": "Fuel Refinery",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.title",
          "label": "Fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/fuel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/fuel.json",
          "text": "Shift-click on the rocket to open its inventory, then place three buckets of fuel into the input slot. Place the other three buckets into the rocket's inventory for a return trip.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/generating_energy",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/generating_energy.json",
          "text": "Generating Energy",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Generating Energy",
          "item_ref_count": 3,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 2
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/generating_energy",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/generating_energy.json",
          "text": "Coal Generator",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.title",
          "label": "Generating Energy",
          "item_ref_count": 3,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 2
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/generating_energy",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/generating_energy.json",
          "text": "Energy is core resource of Ad Astra. It's used in all machines and is required for everything. There are multiple ways to generate energy, the most basic way being the coal generator. It uses burnable resources like wood or coal to generate energy.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Generating Energy",
          "item_ref_count": 3,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 2
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/making_steel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/making_steel.json",
          "text": "Making Steel",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Making Steel",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 3
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/making_steel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/making_steel.json",
          "text": "Etrionic Blast Furnace",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.title",
          "label": "Making Steel",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 3
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/making_steel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/making_steel.json",
          "text": "The Etrionic Blast Furnace is an upgraded version of the blast furnace. It has two modes: blasting and alloying. Blasting works like a blast furnace, but with 4 slots for 4x efficiency. Alloying combines materials to make an alloy.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Making Steel",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 3
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/making_steel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/making_steel.json",
          "text": "Steel is made in an etrionic blast furnace by combining iron and coal. This will make 1 steel ingot per iron and coal. Steel is essential for most recipes and is the main material of your first rocket.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Making Steel",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 3
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/oxygen",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/oxygen.json",
          "text": "At this point, you can launch your rocket; however, you will likely die very fast, as there is no oxygen on the moon. In order to survive on the moon, you will need an oxygenated space suit and an ample oxygen supply.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Oxygen",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/oxygen",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/oxygen.json",
          "text": "To obtain oxygen, it must be extracted from water. An oxygen loader is required to do this. Craft one, then place water in the left tank, and watch it convert into oxygen. You must repeat this process many times until you have enough oxygen.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Oxygen",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/oxygen",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/oxygen.json",
          "text": "Oxygen Loader",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.title",
          "label": "Oxygen",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 5
        }
      ],
      "aliases": []
    },

          "text": "This component can interact with fluid tanks without the need to stop the contraption",

          "label": "Contraption Fluid Exchange",
          "count": 9

        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "create:ponder/portable_fluid_interface",
          "source": "file:minecraft/lang-overlays",

          "source": "file:minecraft/lang-overlays",

        },
        {

          "source": "file:minecraft/lang-overlays",
          "text": "When placed, Stock Links create a new stock network",

          "count": 9
        },
        {

          "source": "file:minecraft/lang-overlays",
          "text": "Right-click an existing link before placing it to bind them",

          "count": 9
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "create:ponder/stock_link",
          "source": "file:minecraft/lang-overlays",
          "text": "Stock-linked packagers make their inventory available to the network",
          "key": "lang-ponder:create.ponder.stock_link.text_3",
          "label": "Logistics Networks and the Stock Link",
          "count": 9
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",

          "source": "file:minecraft/lang-overlays",
          "text": "Other components on the network can now find and request their items",

          "source": "file:minecraft/lang-overlays",
          "text": "Brass Tunnels can be used to cover up your belts",
          "key": "lang-ponder:create.ponder.brass_tunnel.text_1",
          "label": "Using Brass Tunnels",
          "count": 9
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "create:ponder/brass_tunnel",
          "source": "file:minecraft/lang-overlays",
          "text": "Brass Tunnels have filter slots on each open side",
          "key": "lang-ponder:create.ponder.brass_tunnel.text_2",
          "label": "Using Brass Tunnels",
          "count": 9
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "create:ponder/brass_tunnel",
          "source": "file:minecraft/lang-overlays",
          "text": "Filters on inbound connections simply block non-matching items",
          "key": "lang-ponder:create.ponder.brass_tunnel.text_3",
          "label": "Using Brass Tunnels",
          "count": 9
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "create:ponder/brass_tunnel",
          "source": "file:minecraft/lang-overlays",
          "text": "Filters on outbound connections can be used to sort items by type",
          "key": "lang-ponder:create.ponder.brass_tunnel.text_4",
          "label": "Using Brass Tunnels",
          "count": 9
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "create:ponder/brass_tunnel",
          "source": "file:minecraft/lang-overlays",
          "text": "Whenever a passing item has multiple valid exits, the distribution mode will decide how to handle it",

          "label": "Using Brass Tunnels",
          "count": 9

          "source": "file:minecraft/lang-overlays",
          "text": "Brass Tunnels on parallel belts will form a group",

          "label": "Using Brass Tunnels",
          "count": 9

          "id": "create:ponder/brass_tunnel",
          "source": "file:minecraft/lang-overlays",
          "text": "Incoming Items will now be distributed across all connected exits",

    {
      "id": "tfc:support/beams/and/collapses",
      "label": "Support Beams & Collapses",
      "origin": "namespace_generated",
      "confidence": 0.7,
      "support": 17,
      "evidence": [
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/support_beams",
          "confidence": 0.7
        }
      ],
      "semantic_evidence": [
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/support_beams",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/support_beams.json",
          "text": "Support Beams & Collapses",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Support Beams & Collapses",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 9
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/support_beams",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/support_beams.json",
          "text": "In TerraFirmaCraft, raw rock is unstable and susceptible to Collapsing. Many rock blocks, including Raw Rock, Ores, Smooth and Spikes can all rain down on your head under the right circumstances. Support Beams can be used to prevent collapses from occurring.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Support Beams & Collapses",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 9
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/support_beams",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/support_beams.json",
          "text": "Support Beams",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.title",
          "label": "Support Beams & Collapses",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 9
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/support_beams",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/support_beams.json",
          "text": "Collapses can occur whenever a player mines any Raw Rock that is near to Unsupported Raw Rock. Once a collapse has started, however, even previously Supported rock can start to collapse. The rock on the roof of caves is Naturally Supported. Any raw rock with a non-collapsible solid block beneath it, is also Supported. Alternatively, Support Beams can support a wide area at once.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Support Beams & Collapses",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 9
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/support_beams",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/support_beams.json",
          "text": "When graded Ore (ore that can be poor, normal, or rich) collapses, it degrades in quality. Rich ore will become normal, normal will become poor, and poor will turn to cobblestone. Mineral ores will turn into cobblestone right away.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Support Beams & Collapses",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 9
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/support_beams",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/support_beams.json",
          "text": "Dirt, grass, clay, gravel, cobblestone, and sand are also affected by gravity. However, unlike vanilla gravity blocks, these blocks fall down slopes, and cannot be stacked more than one high without supporting blocks around. Ore Deposits also landslide, but do not degrade in quality.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Support Beams & Collapses",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 9
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/support_beams",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/support_beams.json",
          "text": "Support Beams",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.title",
          "label": "Support Beams & Collapses",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 9
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/support_beams",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/support_beams.json",
          "text": "To get started, Support Beams can be crafted with a Saw and any type of Logs. Placing a Support Beam on top of a block places a column up to three tall. These must have a solid block beneath them to stay upright.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Support Beams & Collapses",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 9
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/support_beams",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/support_beams.json",
          "text": "Horizontal beams can be placed between to connect two Vertical beams that are within five blocks, as in the above diagram.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "Support Beams & Collapses",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 9
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/support_beams",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/support_beams.json",
          "text": "Only Horizontal Support Beams cause nearby blocks to be Supported. Any block within a 9 x 5 x 9 area centered on a horizontal support beam is considered Supported. In addition to being supported by support beams, rock can be supported simply by the virtue of having a solid block below it, such as more rock. However, it is important to note that Non Solid Blocks such as Stairs and Slabs, along with Smooth Stone, do not count as supporting.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.6.text",
          "label": "Support Beams & Collapses",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 9
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/support_beams",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/support_beams.json",
          "text": "In addition to Wooden Support Beams TFG also adds higher tiers of support beams. Including Stone Support Beams, Concrete Support Beams, and Steel Support Beams. With the highest tier of supports holding an area of 33 x 13 x 33 blocks centered on the horizontal support beam!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.7.text",
          "label": "Support Beams & Collapses",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 9
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/support_beams",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/support_beams.json",
          "text": "Finally, it is important to know that Chiseling has the potential to cause collapses, just as easily as mining does, when it is done on any Raw Rock that has potential to cause a nearby collapse. Remember kids: practice safe mining!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.8.text",
          "label": "Support Beams & Collapses",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 9
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel.json",
          "text": "Better Fuel",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Better Fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel.json",
          "text": "Cryo Freezer",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.title",
          "label": "Better Fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel.json",
          "text": "Fuel is hard to get; it requires venturing out to the ocean, looking for oil spouts. The Cryo Freezer introduces a new way to obtain a better fuel - Cryo Fuel. Cryo Fuel is much more efficient than refined fuel, allowing you to launch a rocket with just one bucket.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Better Fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel.json",
          "text": "It's created by collecting ice shards, which are found as an ore underground on cold planets. The ice shard then needs to be inserted into a Cryo Freezer, and it will convert to 25 mB of Cryo Fuel. Alternatively, you can convert ice, packed ice and blue ice to fuel. Once you've made at least two buckets (one for a launch and the other for a return trip), place the buckets in the rocket fuel slot.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Better Fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel.json",
          "text": "Cryo Fuel",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.title",
          "label": "Better Fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel.json",
          "text": "In addition to being an excellent fuel, cryo fuel has some other features. When placed, it will damage anything inside, similar to lava. It also freezes water, allowing for an infinite ice source.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Better Fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/detection",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/detection.json",
          "text": "Oxygen Sensor",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.title",
          "label": "Detection",
          "item_ref_count": 3,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 2
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/detection",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/detection.json",
          "text": "The oxygen sensor is a block that will emit a redstone signal when it detects oxygen. You can shift-right-click it to invert the signal. Despite its name, you can also change its detection type by right-clicking with a wrench. It has the option to detect oxygen, safe temperature, and normal gravity.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Detection",
          "item_ref_count": 3,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 2
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/energizer",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/energizer.json",
          "text": "The energizer is a machine capable of powering items and storing large amounts of energy. To charge an item, power the energizer and then right-click on the energizer with that item. It stores 2 million energy, making it capable of charging machines for a very long time. It also retains its energy when broken.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Energizer",
          "item_ref_count": 3,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 2
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/flags",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/flags.json",
          "text": "Flags are decorative blocks available in 16 colors. By default, when placed, they will display the face of the flag owner. They can be configured to display a custom image from a URL by right-clicking and entering the URL in the GUI. This will display the internet image onto the flag.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Flags",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 2
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/fluid_pipes",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/fluid_pipes.json",
          "text": "Fluid Pipes",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Fluid Pipes",

          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 4
        },
        {

          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/fluid_pipes",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/fluid_pipes.json",
          "text": "Fluid Pipes",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.title",
          "label": "Fluid Pipes",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/fluid_pipes",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/fluid_pipes.json",
          "text": "Fluid pipes are similar to cables but with fluid transfer instead of energy transfer. To use them, craft some fluid pipes and a wrench. Then, right-click on one of the pipes with the wrench to cycle through the pipe's modes: 'none,' 'normal,' 'insert,' and 'extract.'",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Fluid Pipes",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/fluid_pipes",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/fluid_pipes.json",
          "text": "Pipes with 'insert' set will receive fluid from the network, while pipes with 'extract' set will pump fluid into the network.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Fluid Pipes",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/fluid_pipes",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/fluid_pipes.json",
          "text": "Like cables, fluid pipes also have a duct block for transfering fluids safely in and out of sealed structures.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Fluid Pipes",
          "item_ref_count": 4,

          "count": 4
        },
        {

        {
